  	//Zendesk script
  	$( document.ready ).ready( function() {  
  			
		$('.mobile-menu').on('click', function(){
			$('.nav-overlay').toggleClass('hide-overlay');
			$('body').toggleClass('noscroll');
		});	
      	$( window ).resize(function() {
  			if($( window ).width() > 1080 ) {
              $('body').removeClass('noscroll');
            }
		});
      
		$('.nav-browse-by.solutions').on('click', function(){
          	$('.browse-solutions.customers').addClass('hide-overlay');
          	$('.browse-solutions.resources').addClass('hide-overlay');
			$('.browse-solutions.solutions').removeClass('hide-overlay');
		});		
      	$('.nav-browse-by.resources').on('click', function(){
          	$('.browse-solutions.customers').addClass('hide-overlay');
			$('.browse-solutions.solutions').addClass('hide-overlay');          
          	$('.browse-solutions.resources').removeClass('hide-overlay');
		});	
      	$('.nav-browse-by.customers').on('click', function(){
			$('.browse-solutions.solutions').addClass('hide-overlay');          
          	$('.browse-solutions.resources').addClass('hide-overlay');
          	$('.browse-solutions.customers').removeClass('hide-overlay');
		});	
      	$('.browse-solutions').mouseleave(function() { 
  			$('.browse-solutions.solutions').addClass('hide-overlay');          
          	$('.browse-solutions.resources').addClass('hide-overlay');
          	$('.browse-solutions.customers').addClass('hide-overlay');
		});
		var coll = document.getElementsByClassName("expand");
		var i;
		for (i = 0; i < coll.length; i++) {
			coll[i].addEventListener("click", function() {
				this.classList.toggle("active");
				var content = this.nextElementSibling;
				if (content.style.maxHeight){
					content.style.maxHeight = null;
				} else {
					content.style.maxHeight = (content.scrollHeight + 40)+ "px";
				} 
			});
		}  
	});
</script>

<script>
  var arr = ['s1','s2','s3','s4'];
  	$(document).ready(function() {
    	defaultSwitch();
    	$("input").change(function() {
      		var status = $(this).attr('id');
      		resetSwitch();
      
      		if(status === 's7'){
        		selectAllSwitch();
      		} else {
        		selectSwitch(status);
      		}
  		});
  	});
  	function defaultSwitch() {
    	$('#s4').prop('checked', true);
        $('.s4-img').css('display','block').addClass('animated');
      	$('.s4-text').css('display','block');
        $('.large-body-text.s4').css({'color':'#5ff67b'});
  	}
  	function resetSwitch() {
    	for (i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
          	$('#' + arr[i]).prop('checked', false);
         	$('.'+arr[i]+'-text').css('display','none');
         	$('.'+arr[i]+'-img').css('display','none').removeClass('animated');
          	$('.large-body-text.'+ arr[i]).css({'color':'#ffffff'});
		}
  	}
  	function selectAllSwitch() {
      	$('.s7-img').css('display','block').removeClass('animated');
      	$('.s7-text').css('display','block');
    	for (i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
          	$('#' + arr[i]).prop('checked', true);
          	$('.large-body-text.'+ arr[i]).css({'color':'#5ff67b'});
		}
  	}
  	function selectSwitch(status) {
      		$('#' + status).prop('checked', true);
    		$('.'+ status +'-img').css('display','block').addClass('animated');
      		$('.'+ status +'-text').css('display','block');
          	$('.large-body-text.'+ status).css({'color':'#5ff67b'});
  	} 
</script>
